req.postDataJSON() : undefined\n+    calls.push({ path, method: req.method(), body })\n+    const ok = data => route.fulfill({ json: { code: '200', data } })\n+    if (path === '/courses') return ok({ records: [{ id: 1, title: '人工智能基础', teacherId: 7 }], total: 1 })\n+    if (path === '/courses/1/chapters') return ok([{ id: 2, title: '机器学习概述' }])\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/knowledge-bases') return ok([{ id: 'kb1', name: '人工智能课件' }])\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/knowledge-bases/kb1/documents') return ok([{ id: 'doc1', title: '第2章.pdf', document_version: 1, page_count: 36 }])\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/graphs' && req.method() === 'GET') return ok(empty ? [] : [graph])\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/graphs' && req.method() === 'POST') {\n+      graph = { ...graph, ...body, status: 'REVIEW_REQUIRED', publishedVersion: 0, graphVersion: 0, nodes: [], edges: [] }\n+      return ok(graph)\n+    }\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/graphs/g1/sources') return ok({ chunks: [source] })\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/graphs/g1/draft') {\n+      graph = { ...graph, ...body, revision: body.revision + 1, status: 'REVIEW_REQUIRED' }\n+      return ok(graph)\n+    }\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/graphs/g1/publish') return ok({ ...graph, status: 'PROJECTING', jobId: 'j1' })\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/graphs/g1/jobs/j1') {\n+      graph = { ...graph, status: 'PUBLISHED', graphVersion: 2, publishedVersion: 2 }\n+      return ok({ status: 'DONE' })\n+    }\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/graphs/g1/evidence/chunk1') return ok(source)\n+    if (path.includes('/neighbors')) return ok({ nodes: [nodes[0], nodes[2]], edges: [{ ...edges[0], id: 'task2', target: 'regression' }], truncated: false })\n+    if (path.includes('/paths')) return ok({ nodes: nodes.slice(0, 2), edges, truncated: false })\n+    if (path === '/api/rag/graphs/g1') {\n+      if (unavailable) return route.fulfill({ status: 503, json: { code: '503', msg: '图谱服务暂不可用' } })\n+      return ok(graph)\n+    }\n+    return ok([])\n+  })\n+  await page.goto('/chapter-knowledge-graph?courseId=1')\n+  return calls\n+}\n+\n+test('official graph reads API nodes and evidence, expands neighbours and searches aliases', async ({ page }) => {\n+  const calls = await setup(page)\n+  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: '课程知识图谱', exact: true })).toBeVisible()\n+  await expect(page.getByTestId('knowledge-network').locator('canvas').first()).toBeVisible()\n+  await page.getByLabel('搜索知识点').fill('有监督')\n+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '定位监督学习', exact: true }).click()\n+  await expect(page.getByTestId('graph-detail')).toContainText('利用有标签样本学习')\n+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '展开相邻知识', exact: true }).click()\n+  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: '查看关系监督学习 → 回归', exact: true })).toBeVisible()\n+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '查看关系监督学习 → 分类', exact: true }).click()\n+  await expect(page.getByTestId('graph-detail')).toContainText('监督学习包括分类任务')\n+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '查看来源chunk1', exact: true }).click()\n+  await expect(page.getByTestId('evidence-detail')).toContainText('第 31 页')\n+  await expect(page.getByTestId('evidence-detail')).toContainText(source.content)\n+  expect(calls.some(call => call.path.endsWith('/neighbors'))).toBe(true)\n+  expect(calls.some(call => call.path.endsWith('/evidence/chunk1'))).toBe(true)\n+})\n+\n+test('teacher creates a chapter draft, imports real source ids, saves and publishes', async ({ page }) => {\n+  const calls = await setup(page, { empty: true })\n+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '新建章节图谱', exact: true }).click()\n+  await page.getByLabel('图谱标题').fill('第2章知识网络')\n+  await page.getByLabel('所属章节').selectOption('2')\n+  await page.getByLabel('资料库').selectOption('kb1')\n+  await page.getByLabel('来源资料').selectOption('doc1')\n+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '创建草稿', exact: true }).click()\n+  await expect(page.getByTestId('graph-editor')).toBeVisible()\n+  await expect(page.getByTestId('graph-sources')).toContainText('chunk1')\n+  await page.getByLabel('实体与关系 JSON').fill(JSON.stringify({ nodes, edges }))\n+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '保存草稿', }).click()\n+  await expect(page.getByText('图谱已发布')).toBeVisible()\n+  expect(calls.find(call => call.method === 'POST' && call.path === '/api/rag/graphs').body).toMatchObject({ courseId: 1, page.getByLabel('仅看已审核先修关系').check()\n+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '查看路径', exact: true }).click()\n+  await expect(page.getByText('已高亮查询路径')).toBeVisible()\n+  expect(calls.some(call => Factsheet\\nL78:   * A fully featured graph library written in pure JS\\nL79:   * Permissive open source license (MIT) for the core Cytoscape.js library and all first-party extensions\\nL80:   * Used in commercial projects and open-source projects in production\\nL81:   * Designed for users first, for both frontfacing app usecases and developer usecases\\nL82:   * Highly optimised\\nL83:   * No external dependencies\\nL84:   * Compatible with\\nL85:     * All modern browsers\\nL86:     * Legacy browsers with ES5 and canvas support\\nL87:       * ES5 and canvas support are required, and feature detection is used for optional performance enhancements.\\nL88:       * Browsers circa 2012 support ES5 fully: IE10, Chrome 23, Firefox 21, Safari 6 (【39†caniuse†caniuse.com】)\\nL89:       * Browsers with partial but sufficient ES5 support also work, such as IE9 and Firefox 4.\\nL90:       * The documentation and examples are not optimised for old browsers, although the library itself is. Some demos may not work in old browsers in order to keep the demo code simple.\\nL91:     * Module systems\\nL92:       * ES modules\\nL93:       * UMD\\nL94:         * CommonJS/Node.js\\nL95:         * Globals\\nL96:         * AMD/Require.js\\nL97:     * Package managers\\nL98:       * npm\\nL99:       * yarn\\nL100:       * bower\\nL101:       * jspm\\nL102:   * Supports the 【40†R language†www.r-project.org】 via 【41†RCyjs†www.bioconductor.org】\\nL103:   * Supports rendering images of graphs on Node.js with 【42†Cytosnap†github.com】\\nL104:   * Has a large suite of tests that can be run in the browser or the terminal\\nL105:   * Documentation includes live code examples, doubling as an interactive requirements specification; example graphs may also be freely modified in your browser’s JS console\\nL106:   * Fully serialisable and deserialisable via JSON\\nL107:   * Uses layouts for automatically or manually positioning nodes\\nL108:   * Supports selectors for terse filtering and graph querying\\nL109:   * Uses stylesheets to separate presentation from data in a rendering agnostic manner\\nL110:   * Abstracted and unified touch events on top of a familiar event model\\nL111:   * Builtin support for standard gestures on both desktop and touch\\nL112:   * Chainable for convenience\\nL113:   * Supports functional programming patterns\\nL114:   * Supports set theory operations\\nL115:   * Includes graph theory algorithms, from BFS to PageRank\\nL116:   * Animatable graph elements and viewport\\nL117:   * Fully extendable (and extensions can be autoscaffolded for you)\\nL118:   * Well maintained\\nL119:     * Weekly patch releas

<button aria-label=\"放大图谱\" @click=\"zoomBy(1.25)\">＋</button>\n+      <button aria-label=\"缩小图谱\" @click=\"zoomBy(0.8)\">−</button>\n+      <button @click=\"fit\">适应视图</button>\n+      <button @click=\"layout\">重新布局</button>\n+    </div>\n+    <span class=\"canvas-instruction\">拖动节点 · 滚轮缩放 · 拖动画布平移</span>\n+  </div>\n+</template>\n+\n+<script setup>\n+import { onBeforeUnmount, onMounted, ref(null)\n+let cy, observer\n+const nodeId = id => `node:${id}`\n+const edgeId = id => `edge:${id}`\n+const colour = type => ({ CONCEPT: '#5074cf', TASK: '#13a69a', ALGORITHM: '#9470d6', MODEL: '#d28d38', METRIC: '#dc668a' }[type] || '#69869d')\n+\n+function syncElements() {\n+  if (!cy) return\n+  const firstLoad = cy.nodes().empty()\n+  const wanted = new Set([...props.nodes.map(node => nodeId(node.id)), ...props.edges.map(edge => edgeId(edge.id))])\n+  cy.batch(() => {\n+    cy.elements().filter(element => !wanted.has(element.id())).remove()\n+    for (const node of props.nodes) {\n+      const id = nodeId(node.id), current = cy.getElementById(id)\n+      const data = { ...node, id, originalId: node.id, colour: colour(node.type) }\n+      if (current.length) current.data(data)\n+      else cy.add({ group: 'nodes', data, position: { x: 80 + Math.random() * 280, y: 80 + Math.random() * 280 } })\n+    }\n+    for (const edge of props.edges) {\n+      const id = edgeId(edge.id), current = cy.getElementById(id)\n+      const data = { ...edge, id, originalId: edge.id, source: nodeId(edge.source), target: nodeId(edge.target) }\n+      if (!cy.getElementById(data.source).length || !cy.getElementById(data.target).length) continue\n+      if (current.length && (current.data('source') !== data.source || current.data('target') !== data.target)) current.remove()\n+      const existing = cy.getElementById(id)\n+      if (existing.length) existing.data(data)\n+      else cy.add({ group: 'edges', data })\n+    }\n+  })\n+  if   cy.edges().forEach(edge => edge.toggleClass('filtered', Boolean(props.relationType && edge.data('type') !== props.relationType)))\n+    if (props.selectedId) {\n+      const selected = cy.getElementById(nodeId(props.selectedId))\n+      if (selected.length) {\n+        selected.addClass('chosen')\n+        cy.elements().difference(selected.closedNeighborhood()).addClass('dimmed')\n+      } else cy.getElementById(edgeId(props.selectedId)).addClass('chosen')\n+    }\n+    const path = new Set(props.pathIds)\n+    cy.elements().forEach(element => element.toggleClass('path', path.has(element.data('originalId'))))\n+  })\n+}\n+function layout() { cy?.layout({ name: 'cose', animate: false, padding: 70, nodeRepulsion: () => 800000, idealEdgeLength: () => 180, gravity: 0.5, randomize: true }).run() }\n+function fit() { cy?.fit(cy.elements(':visible'), 70) }\n+function zoomBy(factor) {\n+  if (cy) cy.zoom({ level: Math.max(0.15, Math.min(3, cy.zoom() * factor)), renderedPosition: { x: cy.width() / 2, y: cy.height() / 2 } })\n+}\n+function focus(id) {\n+  const node = cy?.getElementById(nodeId(id))\n+  if (node?.length) cy.animate({ center: { eles: node }, duration: 180 })\n+}\n+defineExpose({ focus })\n+onMounted(() => {\n+  cy = cytoscape({\n+    container: container.value, elements: [], minZoom: 0.15, maxZoom: 3, wheelSensitivity: 0.25,\n+    style: [\n+      { selector: 'node', style: { shape: 'ellipse', width: 68, height: 68, 'background-color': 'data(colour)', label: 'data(name)', color: '#263750', 'font-size': 14, 'font-family': 'sans-serif', 'text-valign': 'bottom', 'text-margin-y': 9, 'text-wrap': 'wrap', 'text-max-width': 120, 'border-width': 4, 'border-color': '#fff' } },\n+      { selector: 'edge', style: { width: 1.7, 'curve-style': 'bezier', 'line-color': '#aabbd4', 'target-arrow-color': '#8d9eb7', 'target-arrow-shape': 'triangle', 'arrow-scale': 1.1, label: 'data(label)', color: '#566985', 'font-size': 12, 'text-background-color': '#f6f8fc', 'text-background-opacity': 0.95, 'text-background-padding': 4, 'text-rotation': 'autorotate' } },\n+      { selector: 'edge[type = \"PREREQUISITE_FOR\"]', style: { 'line-style': 'dashed', 'line-color': '#a17cc4', 'target-arrow-color': '#a17cc4' } },\n+      { selector: '.chosen', style: { 'border-color': '#1e3f84', 'border-width': 5, 'line-color': '#3156a4', 'target-arrow-color': '#3156a4', width: 3 } },\n+      { selector: '.dimmed', style: { opacity: 0.23 } },\n+      { selector: '.path', style: { 'border-color': '#c07c18', 'line-color': '#c07c18', 'target-arrow-color': '#c07c18', 'border-width': 5, opacity: 1 } },\n+      { selector: '.filtered', style: { display: 'none' } },\n+    ],\n+  })\n+  cy.on('tap', 'node', event => emit('node-select', event.target.data('originalId')))\n+  cy.on('tap', 'edge', event => emit('edge-select', event.target.data('originalId')))\n+  observer = new ResizeObserver(() => cy?.resize())\n+  observer.observe(container.value)\n+  syncElements()\n+})\n+watch(() => [props.nodes, props.edges], syncElements, { deep: true })\n+watch(() => [props.selectedId, props.relationType, props.pathIds], syncStyles, { deep: true })\n+onBeforeUnmount(() => { observer?.disconnect(); cy?.destroy(); cy = null })\n+</script>\n+\n+<style scoped>\n+.network-wrap { position:relative; min-width:0;     return get(id, true);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    public List<Map<String, Object>> list(Integer courseId) {\\r\\n        RagActor actor = authorization.currentActor();\\r\\n        if (courseId == result.put(\\\"edges\\\", slice.edges()); result.put(\\\"truncated\\\", slice.truncated());\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        result.putIfAbsent(\\\"truncated\\\", false);\\r\\n        result.put(\\\"evidence\\\", selectedSources(row));\\r\\n        authorized(row, actor);\\r\\n        return result;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    public Map<String, Object> sources(String id) {\\r\\n        RagActor actor = authorization.currentActor();\\r\\n        Map<String, Object> row = authorized(id, actor);\\r\\n        requireTeacher(actor, number(row, \\\"course_id\\\"));\\r\\n        List<Map<String, Object>> sources = selectedSources(row);\\r\\n        authorized(row, actor);\\r\\n        return Map.of(\\\"chunks\\\", sources);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    public String beginExtraction(String id, int revision, String model, String promptVersion, String fingerprint) {\\r\\n        RagActor actor = authorization.currentActor();\\r\\n        Map<String, Object> row = authorized(id, actor);\\r\\n        requireTeacher(actor, number(row, \\\"course_id\\\"));\\r\\n        if (number(row, \\\"revision\\\") != revision || \\\"PROJECTING\\\".equals(row.get(\\\"state\\\"))) throw conflict(\\\"草稿已变化或正在发布\\\");\\r\\n        String taskId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();\\r\\n        db.update(\\\"INSERT INTO native_graph_extraction(id,graph_id,input_revision,actor_id,model_name,prompt_version,source_fingerprint,status) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'PROCESSING')\\\",\\r\\n                taskId, id, revision, actor.userId(), GraphSnapshotValidator.text(model, 200), promptVersion, fingerprint);\\r\\n        return taskId;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    public void finishExtraction(String taskId, Integer outputRevision, String error) {\\r\\n        db.update(\\\"UPDATE native_graph_extraction SET output_revision=?,status=?,error_message=?,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id=?     text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: D:/ai-learning-collaboration-verified/ai-learning-frontend/src/components/knowledge/KnowledgeGraphCanvas.vue\n@@\n function layout() {\n+  if (!cy) return\n+  const count = cy.nodes().length\n+  cy.nodes().forEach((node, index) => {\n+    const angle = (index / Math.max(count, 1)) * Math.PI * 2\n+    node.position({ x: 300 + 240 * Math.cos(angle), y: 300 + 240 * Math.sin(angle) })\n+  })\n   cy?.layout({\n     name: 'cose',\n     animate: false,\n-    padding: 70,\n-    nodeRepulsion: () => 800000,\n-    idealEdgeLength: () => 180,\n-    gravity: 0.5,\n-    randomize: true,\n+    padding: 40,\n+    nodeRepulsion: () => 12000,\n+    idealEdgeLength: () => 55,\n+    gravity: 1,\n+    componentSpacing: 60,\n+    randomize: false,\n   }).run()\n }\n function fit() {\n-  cy?.fit(cy.elements(':visible'), 70)\n+  cy?.fit(cy.elements(':visible'), 40)\n@@\n function focus(id) {\n   const node = cy?.getElementById(nodeId(id))\n-  if (node?.length) cy.animate({ center: { eles: node }, duration: 180 })\n+  if (node?.length) cy.animate({ center: { eles: node }, zoom: Math.max(cy.zoom(), 0.9), duration: 180 })\n }\n-defineExpose({ focus })\n+function focusRelation(id) {\n+  const edge = cy?.getElementById(edgeId(id))\n+  if (edge?.length) cy.animate({ center: { eles: edge.connectedNodes() }, zoom: Math.max(cy.zoom(), 0.9), duration: 180 })\n+}\n+defineExpose({ focus, focusRelation })\n@@\n-          'font-size': 14,\n+          'font-size': 16,\n@@\n-          'font-size': 12,\n+          'font-size': 14,\n*** Update File: D:/ai-learning-collaboration-verified/ai-learning-frontend/src/views/student/ChapterKnowledgeGraph.vue\n@@\n function selectEdge(id) {\n   selectedEdgeId.value = id\n   selectedNodeId.value = ''\n   evidence.value = null\n+  canvas.value?.focusRelation(id)\n }\n*** End Patch"));

Additional live screenshots and layout metrics use the real isolated Spring Boot server and Neo4j published 24-node chapter.'\nv['recordSnapshot']=manifest['source_line_range']\npaths=set(v['sha256'])\npaths.update(str(p.relative_to(root)).replace('\\\\','/') for p in record.rglob('*') if p.is_file() and p.name!='verification.json')\nv['sha256']={p:hashlib.sha256((root/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in sorted(paths)}\n(record/'verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(v,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\\n',encoding='utf-8')\nprint(json.dumps({'tests':20,'filesHashed':len(paths),'records':manifest['record_count'],'sourceLines':manifest['source_line_range']},ensure_ascii=False))\n'@ | python -","max_output_tokens":700}));
